<!-- page 1 -->
NOTICE OF PUBLIC HEARING AND AGENDA
NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS
Tuesday, March 10, 2020 *UPDATED
ONE OR MORE SUPERVISORS MAY ATTEND VIA VIDEO AND/OR TELEPHONICALLY 
The Navajo County Board of Supervisors will hold a meeting open to the public on Tuesday, March 10, 2020, in the Supervisors’
Chamber in the Navajo County Governmental Complex, Holbrook, Arizona. The Board may vote to meet in a closed executive
session to discuss certain matters as indicated on the following agenda. The Board may meet in executive session pursuant to
A.R.S. § 38-431.03(A) (3) for legal advice on any item.
Persons with disabilities who need accommodation to attend or participate in the meeting may contact the Clerk of the Board’s office
at (928) 524-4053; at least 48 hours prior to the meeting so an accommodation can be arranged.
Items on the agenda may be considered out of order at the Chairman’s discretion. A copy of the agenda background material
provided to the Board, with the exception of items to be considered in executive session, is available for public inspection at Navajo
County website, www.navajocountyaz.gov 
9:00 a.m. Call To Order:  Invocation and Pledge of Allegiance
           
1. CALL TO THE PUBLIC: Individuals may address the Board on any relevant issue for up to 5
minutes. At the close of the call to the public, Board members may not respond to any comments
but may respond to criticism, ask staff to review a matter or ask that a matter be placed on a
future agenda.
 
 
2. CONSENT AGENDA: These items are considered to be routine or administrative in nature and
will be approved in a single motion. A Board member may request for any item to be removed
from the consent agenda and considered on the regular agenda:
 
 
a. Minutes: February 25, 2020  
 
b. Warrant & Voucher Report over $1000 February 2020  
 
c. Constable Reports: Holbrook Precinct #1, January; February; Winslow Precinct #2, January;
Snowflake Precinct #3, February; Show Low Precinct #5, February; Pinetop-Lakeside Precinct
#6, February 2020
 
 
d. New Utility Easement for the City of Holbrook swimming pool  
 
e. Constable Ethics Standards and Training Board Grant CNA20-504 for Outside Training-National
Conference Attendance in the amount of $4,848.42
 
 
f. Reissuance of Stale-Dated Checks  
 
g. Contracts signed by County Manager pursuant to Board of Supervisors Resolution: Contract for
Professional Services for Old Courthouse Reroof with Loren V. Sadler Architect, Inc.

<!-- page 2 -->
3. BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:  
 
a. Presentation to Terrell Kinlicheenie, Joseph City High School, for winning the State Wrestling
Championship in his weight class.
 
Presented By: Chairman Jason Whiting
 
b. Presentation to Conner Schweigert, Show Low High School; and Cael Porter, Mogollon High
School, for winning the State Wrestling Championship in their respective weight classes.
 
Presented By: Supervisor Daryl Seymore
 
c. Presentation regarding reallocation of a used Sheriff vehicle to the Pinon CERT team  
Presented By: Raynard Kieyoani
 
d. Presentation and overview of the 2020 Census  
Presented By: Kimberley Robinson
 
4. *NAVAJO COUNTY PUBLIC HEALTH SERVICES DISTRICT: Board of Directors
Session: 
 
 
a. Presentation and update on CoVid-19  
Presented By: Jeff Lee
 
5. PUBLIC WORKS:  
 
a. Consideration and adoption of Resolution Number 12-2020 accepting easement abandonment
and exchange for a 1,200 ft portion of FS 504 across private property owned by the applicant.
 
Presented By: Bill Bess
 
b. Consideration of award of Contract #B20-01-008 Supply of Aggregate for Cover Material to
Perkins Cinders, Inc., Brimhall Sand, Rock & Building Materials, Inc., and Hatch Construction &
Paving, Inc. effective March 26, 2020.
 
Presented By: John Osgood
 
c. Consideration of award of contract #B20-01-009 Supply of Various Roadway Materials to
Perkins Cinders, Inc., Hatch Construction and Paving, Inc., Brimhall Sand and Rock and
Building Materials, Inc., and Perkins Aggregates, Inc. effective March 28, 2020.
 
Presented By: John Osgood
 
6. COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE: Report from County Manager, County Attorney and Board
members.
 
 
7. ADJOURN  
 
Dated: March 9, 2020 NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS 
Posted: 8:25 a.m. By: MB                         
Melissa W. Buckley - Clerk of the Board

Board of Supervisors Regular 2. d.        
Meeting Date: 03/10/2020  
Title: New Utility Easement for City of Holbrook swimming pool
Submitted For: John Osgood, Public Works Director 
Submitted By: Bill Bess, County Engineer/Assistant Public Works Director
Department: Public Works
Motion before the Board:
New Utility Easement for the City of Holbrook swimming pool
Background:
The City of Holbrook's swimming pool is located on east side of the Navajo County Fair Grounds
property.  The existing under ground electric service has failed.  This new easement provides
a better alignment for the new underground conduit and conductor. 
Attachments
APS easement 
Holbrook Swimming pool map

UTILITY EASEMENT 
NAVAJO COUNTY, a political subdivision of the State of Arizona, (hereinafter called “Grantor”), 
is the owner of the following described real property located in Navajo County, Arizona (hereinafter called 
“Grantor’s Property”): 
SEE EXHIBIT “A” ATTACHED HERETO AND MADE A PART HEREOF 
Grantor, for and in consideration of One Dollar ($1.00) and other valuable consideration, receipt of 
which is hereby acknowledged, does hereby grant an d convey to ARIZONA PUBLIC SERVICE 
COMPANY, an Arizona corporation, (hereinafter called “Grantee”), and to its successors and assigns, a 
non-exclusive right, privilege, and easement at locations and elevations, in, upon, over, under, through and 
across, a portion of Grantor’s Property described as follows (herein called the “Easement Premises”): 
SEE EXHIBIT “B” ATTACHED HERETO AND MADE A PART HEREOF 
Grantee is hereby granted the right within the Easement Premises to:  construct, reconstruct, replace, 
repair, operate and maintain electrical lines, together with appur tenant facilities and fixtures for use in 
connection therewith, for the transmissio n an d distribution of electricity to, through, across, and beyond 
Grantor's Property; install, operate an d maintain teleco mmunication wires, cables, conduits, fixtures and 
facilities incidental to supplying electricity or for Grantee's own use (said electrical and telecommunication 
lines, facilities and fixtures collectively herein called "Grantee Facilities"); utilize the Easement Premises for 
all other purposes connected therewith; and permit the installation of the wires, fixtures, conduits, or cables 
of any other company. 
Grantee is hereby granted the right, but not the obligation, to trim, prune, cut, and clear away trees, 
brush, shrubs, or oth er vegetation on, or adjacent to, the Easement Premises whenever in Grantee’s 
judgment the same shall be necessary for the convenient and safe exercise of the rights herein granted. 
Grantee shall at all times have the right of full and free ingress and egress to and along the Easement 
Premises for the purposes herein specified. 
Page 1 of 6

<!-- page 404 -->
Grantor shall maintain clear areas that extend:  1) 3 feet from and around all edges of all switching 
cabinet pads and 2 feet from and around all edges of all transformer pads and other equipment pads, and 2) a 
clear operational area that extends 10 feet immediately in front of all transformer , switching cabinet and 
other equipment openings, and 3) a 6 feet by 6 feet hot -stick operating area off the front  left corner of all 
transformers.  No obstructions, trees, shrubs, large landscape rocks, fences, fixtures, or permanent structures 
shall be placed by Grantor within said clear areas; nor shall Grantor install landscape irrigation or sprinkler 
systems within said clear areas.  Landscape irrigation or sprinkler systems installed adjacent to the clear 
areas shall be installed and maintained so that the transformers, switching cabinets or any other equipment 
do not get wet by spray or irrigation. 
Grantor shall not locate, erect or construct, or permit to b e located, erected or constructed, any 
building or other structure or drill any well within the limits of the Easement Premises; nor  shall Grantor 
plant or permit to be planted any trees within the limits of the Easement Premises without the prior written 
consent of Grantee.  However, Grantor reserves the right to use the Easement Premises for purposes that are 
not inconsistent with Grantee’s easement rights herein conveyed and which do not interfere with or 
endanger any of the Grantee Facilities, including, without limitation, granting others the right to use all o r 
portions of the Easement Premises for utility or roadway purposes and constructing improvements within the 
Easement Premises such as paving, sidewalk s, landscaping, and curbing.  Notwithstanding th e foregoing, 
Grantor shall not have the right to lower by more than one foot o r raise by more than two feet the surface 
grade of the Easement Premises, and in no event shall a change in the grade compromise Grantee's minimum 
cover requirements or interfere with Grantee's operation, maintenance or repair. 
Grantee agrees that follo wing any installatio n, excavation, mainten ance, repair, or other work 
performed by Grantee within the Easement Premises, the affected area will be restored by Grantee to as 
close to original condition as is reasonably possible, at the ex pense of Grantee; and that Grantee shall 
indemnify Grantor, to the extent required by law, for any loss, cost or damage incurred by Grantor as a result 
of any negligent installation, excavation, maintenance, repair or other work performed by Grantee within the 
Easement Premises. 
The easement granted herein shall not be de emed abandoned except upon Grantee’s execution and 
recording of a formal instrument abandoning the easement. 
The covenants and agreements herein set forth shall extend and inure in favor and to the benefit of, 
and shall be binding on the heirs, administrators, executors, successors in ownership and estate, assigns and 
lessees of Grantor and Grantee.

Title: Reissuance of Stale-Dated Checks
Submitted For: Kari Lopez, Treasurer  Submitted By: Scott Flake, Accountant
Department: Treasurer
Motion before the Board:
Reissuance of Stale-Dated Checks
Background:
Treasurer's checks were issued as refunds for overpaid property taxes or for investor payments.
The checks were voided because they had been outstanding for a year or more. The payees were
contacted and are requesting replacement checks. All have completed the required Affidavit of
Duplicate Warrant form. Upon Board approval, new checks will be issued to the following
individuals pursuant to A.R.S. §11-632.

Board of Supervisors Regular 3. b.        
Meeting Date: 03/10/2020  
Title: Conner Schweigert and Cael Porter - Wrestling 
Submitted By: Donna Hurstrom, Deputy Clerk of the Board
Department: Board of Supervisors
Presented By: Supervisor Daryl Seymore
Subject:
Presentation to Conner Schweigert, Show Low High School; and Cael Porter, Mogollon High
School, for winning the State Wrestling Championship in their respective weight classes.
Background: 
Conner Schweigert, Show Low High School, competed at the 2020 AIA State Championships in
Prescott, Arizona, on February 14-15, 2020, and won the 126 lb weight class.  
Cael Porter, Mogollon High School, competed at the 2020 AIA State Championships in Prescott,
Arizona, on February 14-15, 2020, and won the 120 lb weight class.

Board of Supervisors Regular 5. a.        
Meeting Date: 03/10/2020  
Title: Public Easement Extinguishment and Exchange: FS 504 over Private Property
Submitted For: Bill Bess, County Engineer/Assistant Public Works Director 
Submitted By: Bryan Cook, Sr. Engineering Project Manager II
Department: Public Works
Presented By: Bill Bess
Motion before the Board:
Consideration and adoption of Resolution Number 12-2020 accepting easement abandonment
and exchange for a 1,200 ft portion of FS 504 across private property owned by the applicant.
Background:
Wendover Pines, LLC submitted an application for easement exchange and abandonment for a
1,200 ft portion of FS 504 across private property owned by the applicant. Upon examining and
contacting affected parties (USFS and the Salvation Army) and gaining their approval of the
abandonment and exchange; Public Works now proceeds to formalize the process. 
This is a separate from the subdivision process currently being conducted by Wendover Pines,
LLC and the Planning and Zoning Department.
Currently, there exists two separate easements for FS 504 Road across property owned by
Wendover Pines, LLC. The extinguishment part of the application is to extinguish a 40' wide
easement that is not in use and adjacent to the existing and the currently used 80' easement; while
the easement exchange portion of the application is to re-center the easement to coincide with the
centerline of the existing road. 
This is intended to clean documentation and easements associated with the private property so that
the next phase of Subdivision can occur with non-convoluted language and descriptions. As part
of the application Wendover Pines, LLC has provided both legal descriptions of the 40' easement
to be extinguished, the existing and proposed location descriptions of the 80' Public Roadway
Easement, and a map showing each of the easements as it relates to the physical road.
Recommendation: 
Staff recommends approval of motion and authorization of the Chairman to sign the resolution
associated with Wendover Pines, LLC's easement extinguishment and exchange application.
Fiscal Impact
Fiscal Year: 2020

No Fiscal Impact is associated with this request.

RESOLUTION NO.   -20 
  
A RESOLUTION OF THE BOARD OF SUPERVISORS  
OF NAVAJO COUNTY, ARIZONA, EXCHANGE 
A 40’ AND A 80’ PUBLIC EASEMENT FOR PARCEL 207-04-006A AS RECORDED ON 
DOCKET 875, PAGE 808 AND DOCKET 810, PAGE 159 
 
 
WHEREAS, the legal descriptions were recorded in the Office of the Navajo County Recorder in Docket 875 
page 808 and Docket 810 page 159; and 
 
WHEREAS, said legal descriptions describes both a 40-foot easement and 80-foot Easement over and 
across parcels# 207-04-006A; and 
 
WHEREAS, Wendover Pines, LLC are the current owners of record of affected parcels listed above and 
have applied to the Navajo County Board of Supervisors to extinguish said easements dedicated to the Public and 
provide a new 80-foot Public Roadway Easement centered on the existing road in exchange; and 
 
WHEREAS, the Public Works Department has advised, and the Board of Supervisors hereby finds, 
that said easements for exchange will not impact Public roadway needs and may be extinguished as part of 
the easement exchange with the acceptance of the new 80-foot public roadway easement; and 
 
WHEREAS, the Public Works Department has advised, and the Board of Supervisors hereby finds, 
that the proposed extinguishment of said easement has been duly noticed in accordance with the Navajo 
County Road Abandonment Policy; and 
 
WHEREAS, ARS § 28-7210 provides that notwithstanding any such extinguishment, existing rights 
of way and easements for certain utility purposes remain in place, 
 
NOW, THEREFORE, BE IT RESOLVED by the Board of Supervisors of Navajo County as follows: 
 
1. That the 40-foot and 80-foot easement over and across the across PARCEL 207-04-006A is 
hereby EXTINGUISHED. 
 
2. The new 80-foot Public Easement over and across PARCEL 207-04-006A is accepted in 
EXCHANGE. 
 
3. That notwithstanding said extinguishment, existing utility easements shall remain in place pursuant 
to ARS § 28-7210.

Board of Supervisors Regular 5. b.        
Meeting Date: 03/10/2020  
Title: Award Contract #B20-01-008 Supply of Aggregate for Cover Material 
Submitted For: John Osgood, Public Works Director 
Submitted By: Jeanine Carruthers, Deputy Director Administration
Department: Public Works
Presented By: John Osgood
Motion before the Board:
Consideration of award of Contract #B20-01-008 Supply of Aggregate for Cover Material to
Perkins Cinders, Inc., Brimhall Sand, Rock & Building Materials, Inc., and Hatch Construction &
Paving, Inc. effective March 26, 2020.
Background:
This is a annual contract for the Supply of Aggregate for Cover Material.  Sealed proposals were
received by the Board of Supervisors at the Navajo County Government Center, Holbrook,
Arizona until 2:00 p.m. M.S.T., February 26, 2020 in strict accordance with specifications on file
in the office of the Navajo County Department of Public Works.  No bids were accepted after
2:00 p.m. M.S.T.  The bids were publicly opened and read aloud at 2:15 p.m.
Navajo County may award contracts to multiple bidders for each item, and select the bidder at the
time of purchase based on price, convenience, availability, and/or quality.  Four bids were
received and the award of contracts to all but Green Dream International allows adequate coverage
for materials by location.  Green Dream International's material location is located in Cameron,
Arizona and it would not be cost effective for the County to utilize this site.
Recommendation: 
Staff recommends approval.

Board of Supervisors Regular 5. c.        
Meeting Date: 03/10/2020  
Title: Award of Contract B20-01-009 Supply of Various Roadway Materials
Submitted For: John Osgood, Public Works Director 
Submitted By: Eileen Smith, Account Specialist
Department: Public Works
Presented By: John Osgood
Motion before the Board:
Consideration of award of contract #B20-01-009 Supply of Various Roadway Materials to Perkins
Cinders, Inc., Hatch Construction and Paving, Inc., Brimhall Sand and Rock and Building
Materials, Inc., and Perkins Aggregates, Inc. effective March 28, 2020.
Background:
Sealed proposals were received by the Board of Supervisors at the Navajo County Governmental
Center, Holbrook, Arizona until 3:00 p.m. M.S.T, February 26, 2020, in strict accordance with the
specifications on file in the office of the Navajo County Public Works. No bids were accepted
after 3:00 p.m. M.S.T.  The bids were publicly opened and read aloud at 3:15 p.m. This is an
annual "multi-award" contract for roadway materials which are purchased based on price,
convenience, availability and/or quality. The award of contracts to all bidders allows adequate
coverage for road materials by location.
Recommendation: 
The unit prices have increased approximately $1.03 cents per ton for all materials with the
exception of 2 sack slurry and concrete which has an average increase of $3.62 per cubic yard.
The 2 sack slurry and concrete materials are not utilized as often as the other materials. With
these being our local vendors, staff is recommending award of contracts to all bidders.
Fiscal Impact
Fiscal Year: 2020
Budgeted Y/N: Y
Amount Requested:
Fiscal Impact:
The Public Works budget includes funding for road maintenance materials.

THIS CONTRACT is made and entered into as of March 28, 2020 (the 
“Effective Date”) by and between Navajo County (“Coun ty”) and Perkins Cinders, Inc.  
(“Contractor”). 
 
1. SCOPE OF WORK : Contractor shall furnish any and all materials, equipment and 
labor required for performing all work for Supplying Various Roadway Materials, 
Contract #B20-01-009, in accordance with the Contract Documents, in a workmanlike 
manner to the satisfaction of County through its Engineers. 
 
2. CONTRACT DOCUMENTS: The Contract Documents comprise this Contract and the 
attached ☒ Call for Bids, ☐ Specifications, ☐ General Provisions, ☒ Special 
Provisions, ☒ Proposal dated February 17, 2020, ☒ Bidding Schedule, ☒ Bid Bond, 
☐ Performance Bond, ☒ No Collusion Affidavit, ☒ Subcontracting Certification, ☐ 
Plans, and addenda thereto, if any.  If there is any conflict in the documents, this 
Contract shall control over all others; the Specifications shall control over the General 
Provisions and Special Provisions; and the Special Provisions shall control over  the 
General Provisions. 
 
3. NO LIENS / TIME FOR COMPLETION : Contractor shall furnish all materials, 
equipment and labor in accordance with the Contract Documents free and clear of all 
claims, liens and charges whatsoever, within the time, or times, stated in the Proposal. 
 
4. CANCELLATION: This Contract is subject to cancellation pursuant to A.R.S. § 38 -
511, (concerning conflicts of interest). 
 
5. PAYMENTS:  Payment shall be made on the basis of the unit price(s) bid for the 
awarded items in the Bidding Schedule for Contract # B20-01-009.  Contractor shall 
submit invoices to County for all work performed hereunder.  No later than 30 days 
after each invoice has been verified and approved by the Engineer, County shall pay 
to Contractor 100% of the approved amount. 
 
6. CERTIFICATIONS:

for random inspection by County during normal business hours.
